\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-({\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\eta{\bm{g}}_{t-1})\|^{2}-\eta^{2}\|{\bm{g}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}\right]+\frac{L_{s}}{2}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-({\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\eta{\bm{g}}_{t-1})\|^{2}-\frac{\eta}{2}\|{\bm{g}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\left[\frac{L_{s}-\frac{1}{\eta}}{2}\right]\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-({\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\eta{\bm{g}}_{t-1})\|^{2}-(1-\sqrt{\beta})\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}\right]-\frac{\eta}{2}\|{\bm{g}}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left[\frac{L_{s}-\frac{1}{\eta}}{2}\right]\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\eta^{2}\|{\bm{g}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-2\langle\eta{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\rangle\right]-\frac{1}{2\eta}(1-\sqrt{\beta})\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{\eta}{2}\|{\bm{g}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\left[\frac{L_{s}-\frac{1}{\eta}}{2}\right]\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-2\langle\eta{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\rangle\right]-\frac{1}{2\eta}(1-\sqrt{\beta})\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left[\frac{L_{s}-\frac{1}{\eta}}{2}\right]\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\langle{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\rangle-\frac{1}{2\eta}(1-\sqrt{\beta})\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left[\frac{L_{s}-\frac{1}{\eta}+C}{2}\right]\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2C}\|\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1}\|^{2},

where (a) follows from Lemma
3.2
and (b) follows from the inequality
\langle a,b\rangle\leq\frac{C}{2}a^{2}+\frac{1}{2C}b^{2}
, for any
(a,b)\in(\mathbb{R}^{d})^{2}
with
C>0
an arbitrary strictly positive constant.

Let us now assume that
\eta=\frac{1}{L_{s}+C}
: therefore the term
\left[\frac{L_{s}-\frac{1}{\eta}+C}{2}\right]\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}
above is
0
. We now take the conditional expectation (conditioned on
{\textnormal{w}}_{t-1}
, which is the random variable which realizations are
{\bm{w}}_{t-1}
), on both sides, and from Lemma
F.3

we obtain the inequality below (we slightly abuse notations and denote
\mathbb{E}[\cdot|{\textnormal{w}}_{t-1}={\bm{w}}_{t-1}]
by
\mathbb{E}[\cdot|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}]
):

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[R({\bm{w}}_{t})|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}]\leq

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1}),{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\eta}(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]+\frac{B(n-s_{t-1})}{2Cs_{t-1}(n-1)}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\left[R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})-R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-\frac{\nu_{s}}{2}\|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\eta}(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]+\frac{B}{2Cs_{t-1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})+\left[\frac{\frac{1}{\eta}-\nu_{s}}{2}\right]\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta}(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{B}{2Cs_{t-1}},

where (a) follows from the RSC condition, and the fact that
s_{t-1}\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
.

We recall that
\eta=\frac{1}{L_{s}+C}
.
Let us define
\alpha:=\frac{C}{L_{s}}+1
. Then
C=(\alpha-1)L_{s}
, and
\eta=\frac{1}{\alpha L_{s}}
. Also recall that
\kappa_{s}=\frac{L_{s}}{\nu_{s}}
.

We can simplify the inequality above into:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[R({\bm{w}}_{t})|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}]-R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\left(1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}\right)\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{\eta B}{Cs_{t-1}}\right].

We now take the expectation over
{\textnormal{w}}_{t-1}
of the above inequality (i.e. we take
\mathbb{E}_{{\textnormal{w}}_{t-1}}[\cdot]
): using the law of total expectation (
\mathbb{E}[\cdot]=\mathbb{E}_{{\textnormal{w}}_{t-1}}[\mathbb{E}[\cdot|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}]]
) we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}R({\bm{w}}_{t})-R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\left(1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}\right)\mathbb{E}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta B}{Cs_{t-1}}\right]

(18)

Similarly as in
(
Liu & Foygel Barber 2020
)
, we now take a weighted sum over
t=1,...,T
, to obtain:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}2\eta\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\mathbb{E}[R({\bm{w}}_{t})-R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\left[\left(1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}\right)\mathbb{E}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta B}{Cs_{t-1}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\left[\left(1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}\right)\mathbb{E}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\frac{\eta B}{Cs_{t-1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\sqrt{\beta})\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t+1}\mathbb{E}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\frac{\eta B}{Cs_{t-1}}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle(1-\sqrt{\beta})\left[\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}-\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{w}}_{T}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\frac{\eta B}{Cs_{t-1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\sqrt{\beta})\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\frac{\eta B}{Cs_{t-1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\frac{\eta B}{Cs_{t-1}},

(19)

where (a) follows from simplifying the telescopic sum.

We now choose
k
and
s_{t}
as follows: we choose
k\geq 4\alpha^{2}\kappa_{s}^{2}\bar{k}
, which implies that:

\displaystyle\sqrt{\beta}\leq\frac{1}{2\alpha\kappa_{s}}

\displaystyle\implies\sqrt{\beta}\leq\frac{1}{2\alpha\kappa_{s}-1}

\displaystyle\implies 1-\sqrt{\beta}\geq 1-\frac{1}{2\alpha\kappa_{s}-1}=\frac{2\alpha\kappa_{s}-2}{2\alpha\kappa_{s}-1}=\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\frac{1}{2\alpha\kappa_{s}}}

\displaystyle\implies\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)\leq 1-\frac{1}{2\alpha\kappa_{s}}.

(20)

And we choose
s_{t}:=\left\lceil\frac{\tau}{\omega^{t}}\right\rceil
with
\omega:=1-\frac{1}{4\alpha\kappa_{s}}
and
\tau:=\frac{\eta B}{C}
.

Let us call
\nu:=1-\frac{1}{2\alpha\kappa_{s}}
. Note that we have:

\nu\leq\omega.

(21)

And that we have the inequality below:

\frac{\nu}{\omega}=\frac{1-\frac{1}{2\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\frac{1}{4\alpha\kappa_{s}}}=\frac{4\alpha\kappa_{s}-2}{4\alpha\kappa_{s}-1}=1-\frac{1}{4\alpha\kappa_{s}-1}\leq 1-\frac{1}{4\alpha\kappa_{s}}=\omega.

(22)

This allows us to simplify equation
19
into:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\sum_{t=1}^{T}2\eta\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}[R({\bm{w}}_{t})-R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})]

\displaystyle\leq\nu^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\nu^{T-t}\omega^{t-1}

\displaystyle=\nu^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{\omega^{T}}{\omega}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{\nu}{\omega}\right)^{T-t}

\displaystyle=\nu^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{\omega^{T}}{\omega}\frac{1-\left(\frac{\nu}{\omega}\right)^{T}}{1-\left(\frac{\nu}{\omega}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\nu^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{\omega^{T}}{\omega}\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{\nu}{\omega}\right)}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\nu^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{\omega^{T}}{\omega}\frac{1}{1-\omega}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\nu^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\omega^{T}\frac{1}{1-\omega}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}\omega^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\omega^{T}\frac{1}{1-\omega}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}\frac{\omega^{T}}{1-\omega}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\omega^{T}\frac{1}{1-\omega}

\displaystyle=\frac{\omega^{T}}{1-\omega}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)

\displaystyle=4\alpha\kappa_{s}\omega^{T}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right),

where in the left hand side we have used the linearity of expectation, and where (a) uses equation
22
,
(b) uses the fact that
\frac{1}{\omega}=\frac{1}{1-\frac{1}{4\alpha\kappa_{s}}}\leq\frac{1}{1-\frac{1}{4}}=\frac{4}{3}
(since
\kappa_{s}\geq 1
and
\alpha\geq 1
(indeed, from the theorem’s assumption
\alpha=\frac{C}{L_{s}}+1
with
C>0
)), (c) uses equation
21
, and (d) uses the fact that
\omega<1
so
1<\frac{1}{1-\omega}
.

Let us now normalize the above inequality:

\mathbb{E}\frac{\sum_{t=1}^{T}2\eta\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}R({\bm{w}}_{t})}{\sum_{t=1}^{T}2\eta\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}}\leq R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})+\frac{4\alpha\kappa_{s}\omega^{T}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\sum_{t=1}^{T}2\eta\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}}.

The left hand side above is a weighted sum, which is an upper bound on the smallest term of the sum. Regarding the right hand side, we can simplify it using the fact that
0<\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)
, and therefore:

\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\geq 1.

Therefore, we obtain:

\mathbb{E}\min_{t\in\{1,..,T\}}R({\bm{w}}_{t})-R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\leq\frac{4\alpha\kappa_{s}\omega^{T}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{2\eta}=2\alpha^{2}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\omega^{T}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)

Which can be simplified into the expression below, using the definition of
\hat{{\bm{w}}}_{T}
:

\mathbb{E}R(\hat{{\bm{w}}}_{T})-R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\leq 2\alpha^{2}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\omega^{T}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right).

The proof is completed.
∎

Corollary F.4
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
4.2
, let
\varepsilon
be a small enough positive number
\varepsilon>0
. To achieve an error
\mathbb{E}R(\hat{{\bm{w}}}_{T})-R(\bar{\bm{w}})\leq\varepsilon
using Algorithm
2
the number of calls to a gradient
\nabla R_{i}
(#IFO), and the number of hard thresholding operations (#HT) are respectively:

\text{\#HT}=\mathcal{O}(\kappa_{s}\log(\frac{1}{\varepsilon})),\ \ \ \text{\#IFO}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\kappa_{s}}{\nu_{s}\varepsilon}\right).

Proof of Corollary
F.4
.

Let
\varepsilon\in\mathbb{R}_{+}^{*}
.
Let us find
T
to ensure that
\mathbb{E}R(\hat{{\bm{w}}}_{T})-R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\leq\varepsilon
.
This will be enforced if:

\displaystyle 2\alpha^{2}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\omega^{T}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)\leq\varepsilon

\displaystyle\iff T\log(\omega)\leq\log\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{2\alpha^{2}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle\iff T\geq\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{2\alpha^{2}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right).

Therefore, let us take:

T:=\left\lceil\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{2\alpha^{2}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right)\right\rceil.

(23)

We can now derive the #IFO and #HT. First, we have one hard-thresholding operation at each iteration, therefore #HT
=T
. Using the fact that
\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}=\frac{1}{-\log(\omega)}=\frac{1}{-\log(1-\frac{1}{4\alpha\kappa_{s}})}\leq\frac{1}{\frac{1}{4\alpha\kappa_{s}}}=4\alpha\kappa_{s}
(since by property of the logarithm, for all
x\in(-\infty,-1):\log(1-x)\leq-x
), we obtain that
\text{\#HT}=\mathcal{O}(\kappa_{s}\log\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right))
.

We now turn to computing the #IFO. At each iteration
t
we have
s_{t}
gradient evaluations, therefore:

#IFO

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}s_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left(\frac{\tau}{\omega^{t}}+1\right)

\displaystyle=T+\tau\frac{\left(\frac{1}{\omega}\right)^{T}-1}{\frac{1}{\omega}-1}

\displaystyle\leq T+\frac{\tau}{{\frac{1}{\omega}-1}}\left(\frac{1}{\omega}\right)^{T}

\displaystyle=T+\frac{\tau}{{\frac{1}{\omega}-1}}\exp\left(T\log\left(\frac{1}{\omega}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}1+\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{2\alpha^{2}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle~~~~~+\frac{\tau}{{\frac{1}{\omega}-1}}\exp\left(\log\left(\frac{1}{\omega}\right)\left[\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{2\alpha^{2}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right)+1\right]\right)

\displaystyle=1+\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{2\alpha^{2}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right)+\frac{\frac{\tau}{\omega}}{{\frac{1}{\omega}-1}}\frac{2\alpha^{2}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=1+\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{2\alpha^{2}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right)+\frac{\tau}{{1-\omega}}\frac{2\alpha^{2}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=1+\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{2\alpha^{2}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right)+\tau\frac{8\alpha^{3}L_{s}\kappa_{s}^{2}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}1+\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{2\alpha^{2}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle~~~~~+\frac{B}{\alpha L_{s}}\frac{1}{L_{s}(\alpha-1)}\frac{8\alpha^{3}L_{s}}{\varepsilon}\frac{L_{s}}{\nu_{s}}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)

\displaystyle=1+\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{2\alpha^{2}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right)+\frac{8B\alpha^{2}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{(\alpha-1)\nu_{s}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon},

where (a) follows from equation
23
, and for (b) we recall that
\tau=\frac{\eta B}{C}
,
\eta=\frac{1}{\alpha L_{s}}
and
C=L_{s}(\alpha-1)
.

Therefore, overall, the IFO complexity is in
\mathcal{O}(\frac{\kappa_{s}}{\nu_{s}\varepsilon})
.

∎

F.2.3
Proof of Theorem
4.3

We now proceed with the full proof of Theorem
4.3
.

Proof of Theorem
4.3
.

Similary as in the proof of Theorem
4.2
in Section
F.2.2
, let us take:

\eta:=\frac{1}{L_{s}+C}
, and
\alpha:=\frac{C}{L_{s}}+1
. Then
C=(\alpha-1)L_{s}
, and
\eta=\frac{1}{\alpha L_{s}}
. Recall that
\kappa_{s}:=\frac{L_{s}}{\nu_{s}}
. Denote
{\bm{v}}_{t}=\mathcal{H}_{k}({\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\eta\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1}))
for any
t\in\mathbb{N}
.

Similarly as in Section
F.2.2
, the
L_{s}
-smoothness of
R
implies that

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t})\leq

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1}),{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle+\frac{L_{s}}{2}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\langle{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle+\frac{L_{s}}{2}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-({\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\eta{\bm{g}}_{t-1})\|^{2}-\eta^{2}\|{\bm{g}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}\right]+\frac{L_{s}}{2}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-({\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\eta{\bm{g}}_{t-1})\|^{2}-\frac{\eta}{2}\|{\bm{g}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\left[\frac{L_{s}-\frac{1}{\eta}}{2}\right]\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-({\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\eta{\bm{g}}_{t-1})\|^{2}-\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}+\sqrt{\beta}\|{\bm{v}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}\right]-\frac{\eta}{2}\|{\bm{g}}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left[\frac{L_{s}-\frac{1}{\eta}}{2}\right]\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\eta^{2}\|{\bm{g}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-2\langle\eta{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\rangle\right]-\frac{1}{2\eta}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{\sqrt{\beta}}{2\eta}\|{\bm{v}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}-\frac{\eta}{2}\|{\bm{g}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\left[\frac{L_{s}-\frac{1}{\eta}}{2}\right]\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-2\langle\eta{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\rangle\right]-\frac{1}{2\eta}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}+\frac{\sqrt{\beta}}{2\eta}\|{\bm{v}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}

(24)

\displaystyle+\left[\frac{L_{s}-\frac{1}{\eta}}{2}\right]\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\langle{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\rangle-\frac{1}{2\eta}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}+\frac{\sqrt{\beta}}{2\eta}\|{\bm{v}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left[\frac{L_{s}-\frac{1}{\eta}+C}{2}\right]\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2C}\|\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1}\|^{2},

(25)

where (a) follows from Lemma
3.6
and (b) follows from the inequality
\langle a,b\rangle\leq\frac{C}{2}a^{2}+\frac{1}{2C}b^{2}
, for any
(a,b)\in(\mathbb{R}^{d})^{2}
with
C>0
an arbitrary strictly positive constant.
Let us now take
\eta:=\frac{1}{L_{s}+C}
: therefore the term
\left[\frac{L_{s}-\frac{1}{\eta}+C}{2}\right]\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}
above is
0
. We now take the conditional expectation (conditioned on
{\textnormal{w}}_{t-1}
, which is the random variable which realizations are
{\bm{w}}_{t-1}
), on both sides, and from Lemma
F.3

we obtain the inequality below (we slightly abuse notations and denote
\mathbb{E}[\cdot|{\textnormal{w}}_{t-1}={\bm{w}}_{t-1}]
by
\mathbb{E}[\cdot|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}]
):

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[R({\bm{w}}_{t})|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}]\leq

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1}),{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\eta}\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]+\frac{\sqrt{\beta}}{2\eta}\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{v}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]+\frac{B(n-s_{t-1})}{2Cs_{t-1}(n-1)}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\left[R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})-R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-\frac{\nu_{s}}{2}\|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\eta}\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]+\frac{\sqrt{\beta}}{2\eta}\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{v}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]+\frac{B}{2Cs_{t-1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})+\left[\frac{\frac{1}{\eta}-\nu_{s}}{2}\right]\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta}\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]+\frac{\sqrt{\beta}}{2\eta}\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{v}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{B}{2Cs_{t-1}},

(26)

where (a) follows from the RSC condition, and the fact that
s_{t-1}\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
.

Now recall that we have taken
\eta=\frac{1}{L_{s}+C}
, and let us define
\alpha:=\frac{C}{L_{s}}+1
. Then
C=(\alpha-1)L_{s}
, and
\eta=\frac{1}{\alpha L_{s}}
. Also recall that
\kappa_{s}=\frac{L_{s}}{\nu_{s}}
.

We can simplify the inequality above into:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[R({\bm{w}}_{t})|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}]-R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\left(1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}\right)\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]+\sqrt{\beta}\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{v}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]+\frac{\eta B}{Cs_{t-1}}\right].

We now take the expectation over
{\textnormal{w}}_{t-1}
of the above inequality (i.e. we take
\mathbb{E}_{{\textnormal{w}}_{t-1}}[\cdot]
): using the law of total expectation (
\mathbb{E}[\cdot]=\mathbb{E}_{{\textnormal{w}}_{t-1}}[\mathbb{E}[\cdot|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}]]
) we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}R({\bm{w}}_{t})-R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\left(1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}\right)\mathbb{E}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}+\sqrt{\beta}\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{v}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta B}{Cs_{t-1}}\right].

Additionally, in view of equation
18
applied at
{\bm{v}}_{t}
instead of
{\bm{w}}_{t}
, (since
{\bm{v}}_{t}
here corresponds to the
{\bm{w}}_{t}
from Section
F.2.2
, i.e.
{\bm{v}}_{t}
is the hard-thresholding of an iterate after a gradient step), we know that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}R({\bm{v}}_{t})-R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\left(1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}\right)\mathbb{E}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta B}{Cs_{t-1}}\right].

We now take a convex combination similarly as in the case without additional constraint (section
E.2
), for some
\rho\in(0,1)
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}(1-\rho)R({\bm{w}}_{t})+\rho R({\bm{v}}_{t})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\left(1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}\right)\mathbb{E}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-(1-\rho)\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}\right.

\displaystyle~~~~\left.+\left((1-\rho)\sqrt{\beta}-(1-\sqrt{\beta})\rho\right)\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{v}}_{t}-\bar{\bm{w}}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta B}{Cs_{t-1}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\left(1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}\right)\mathbb{E}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-(1-\rho)\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}\right.

\displaystyle~~~~\left.-\left(\rho-\sqrt{\beta}\right)\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{v}}_{t}-\bar{\bm{w}}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta B}{Cs_{t-1}}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\left(1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}\right)\mathbb{E}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-(1-\rho)\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}\right.

\displaystyle~~~~\left.-\left(\rho-\sqrt{\beta}\right)\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{\bm{w}}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta B}{Cs_{t-1}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\left(1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}\right)\mathbb{E}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta B}{Cs_{t-1}}\right],

where in (b), we have assumed that
\sqrt{\beta}\leq\rho
(later we will verify that our choice of
k
ensures such a condition), and have used the fact that projection onto a convex set is non-expansive (which implies that
\|{\bm{v}}_{t}-\bar{\bm{w}}\|^{2}\geq\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{\bm{w}}\|^{2}
). Similarly as in
F.2.2
, we now take a weighted sum over
t=1,...,T
, to obtain:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}2\eta\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\mathbb{E}[(1-\rho)R({\bm{w}}_{t})+\rho R({\bm{v}}_{t})-R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\left[\left(1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}\right)\mathbb{E}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta B}{Cs_{t-1}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\left[\left(1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}\right)\mathbb{E}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\frac{\eta B}{Cs_{t-1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\sqrt{\beta})\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t+1}\mathbb{E}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\frac{\eta B}{Cs_{t-1}}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle(1-\sqrt{\beta})\left[\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}-\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{w}}_{T}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\frac{\eta B}{Cs_{t-1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\sqrt{\beta})\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\frac{\eta B}{Cs_{t-1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\frac{\eta B}{Cs_{t-1}},

(27)

where (a) follows from simplifying the telescopic sum.

We now choose
k
and
s_{t}
as follows: we choose
k\geq 4\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\alpha^{2}\kappa_{s}^{2}\bar{k}
, which implies that:

\rho\geq\sqrt{\beta}
(thereby verifying the assumption made earlier), and that:

\displaystyle\sqrt{\beta}\leq\frac{1}{2\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}}

\displaystyle\implies\sqrt{\beta}\leq\frac{1}{2\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}-1}

\displaystyle\implies 1-\sqrt{\beta}\geq 1-\frac{1}{2\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}-1}=\frac{2\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}-2}{2\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}-1}=\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\frac{1}{2\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}}}\overset{(a)}{\geq}\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\frac{1}{2\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}}}

\displaystyle\implies\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)\leq 1-\frac{1}{2\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}},

(28)

where (a) follows from the fact that
\rho\leq 1
.

And we now choose
s_{t}:=\left\lceil\frac{\tau}{\omega^{t}}\right\rceil
, with
\omega:=1-\frac{1}{4\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}}
and
\tau:=\frac{\eta B}{C}
.

Let us call
\nu:=1-\frac{1}{2\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}}
. Note that we have:

\nu\leq\omega.

(29)

And that we have the inequality below:

\frac{\nu}{\omega}=\frac{1-\frac{1}{2\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\frac{1}{4\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}}}=\frac{4\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}-2}{4\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}-1}=1-\frac{1}{4\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}-1}\leq 1-\frac{1}{4\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}}=\omega.

(30)

This allows us to simplify equation
27
into:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\sum_{t=1}^{T}2\eta\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}[(1-\rho)R({\bm{w}}_{t})+\rho R({\bm{v}}_{t})-R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})]

\displaystyle\leq\nu^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\nu^{T-t}\omega^{t-1}

\displaystyle=\nu^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{\omega^{T}}{\omega}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{\nu}{\omega}\right)^{T-t}

\displaystyle=\nu^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{\omega^{T}}{\omega}\frac{1-\left(\frac{\nu}{\omega}\right)^{T}}{1-\left(\frac{\nu}{\omega}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\nu^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{\omega^{T}}{\omega}\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{\nu}{\omega}\right)}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\nu^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{\omega^{T}}{\omega}\frac{1}{1-\omega}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\nu^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\omega^{T}\frac{1}{1-\omega}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}\omega^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\omega^{T}\frac{1}{1-\omega}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}\frac{\omega^{T}}{1-\omega}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\omega^{T}\frac{1}{1-\omega}

\displaystyle=\frac{\omega^{T}}{1-\omega}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)

\displaystyle=4\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}\omega^{T}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right),

where in the left hand side we have used the linearity of expectation, and where (a) uses equation
30
,
(b) uses the fact that
\frac{1}{\omega}=\frac{1}{1-\frac{1}{4\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}}}\leq\frac{1}{1-\frac{1}{4}}=\frac{4}{3}
(since
\kappa_{s}\geq 1
and
\alpha\geq 1
(indeed, from the theorem’s assumption
\alpha=\frac{C}{L_{s}}+1
with
C>0
), so consequently
\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\geq 1
), (c) uses equation
29
, and (d) uses the fact that
\omega<1
so
1<\frac{1}{1-\omega}
.

Let us now normalize the above inequality:

\mathbb{E}\frac{\sum_{t=1}^{T}2\eta\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}(1-\rho)R({\bm{w}}_{t})+\rho R({\bm{v}}_{t})}{\sum_{t=1}^{T}2\eta\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}}\leq R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})+\frac{4\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}\omega^{T}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\sum_{t=1}^{T}2\eta\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}}.

The left hand side above is a weighted sum, which is an upper bound on the smallest term of the sum.

Regarding the right hand side, we can simplify it using the fact that
0<\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)
, and therefore:

\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\geq 1.

Therefore, we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\min_{t\in\{1,..,T\}}(1-\rho)R({\bm{w}}_{t})+\rho R({\bm{v}}_{t})-R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}\omega^{T}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{2\eta}

\displaystyle=2\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{\rho}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\omega^{T}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right).

(31)

We denote by
\varepsilon_{T}
the right-hand side above:

\varepsilon_{T}=2\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{\rho}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\omega^{T}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right).

We now proceed similarly as in the proof of Theorem
3.7
above. Recall that we have assumed in the Assumptions of Theorem
4.3
, without loss of generality, that
R
is non-negative, which implies that
R\left({\bm{v}}_{t}\right)\geq 0
. Plugging this in equation
F.2.3
implies that:

\mathbb{E}\min_{t\in[T]}R\left({\bm{w}}_{t}\right)\leq\frac{1}{1-\rho}R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})+\frac{\varepsilon_{T}}{1-\rho}\leq(1+2\rho)R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})+\frac{\varepsilon_{T}}{1-\rho}.

(32)

Plugging the change of variable
\varepsilon^{\prime}_{T}=\frac{\varepsilon_{T}}{1-\rho}
into equation
32
above, we obtain that:

\mathbb{E}\min_{t\in[T]}R\left({\bm{w}}_{t}\right)\leq(1+2\rho)R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})+\varepsilon_{T}^{\prime}.

Further, consider an ideal case where
\bar{{\bm{w}}}
is a global minimizer of
R
over
\mathcal{B}_{0}(k):=

\left\{{\bm{w}}:\|{\bm{w}}\|_{0}\leq k\right\}
. Then
R\left({\bm{v}}_{t}\right)\geq R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})
is always true for all
t\geq 1
. It follows that the bound in equation
32
yields:

\mathbb{E}\min_{t\in[T]}\left\{(1-\rho)R\left({\bm{w}}_{t}\right)+\rho R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\right\}\leq\mathbb{E}\min_{t\in[T]}\left\{(1-\rho)R\left({\bm{w}}_{t}\right)+\rho R\left({\bm{v}}_{t}\right)\right\}\leq R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})+\varepsilon_{T},

which implies:
\mathbb{E}\min_{t\in[T]}R\left({\bm{w}}_{t}\right)\leq R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})+\frac{\varepsilon_{T}}{1-\rho}
.
In this case, we can simply set
\rho=0.5
, and define
\varepsilon^{\prime}_{T}=\frac{\varepsilon_{T}}{1-\rho}=2\varepsilon_{T}
similarly as above.. The proof is completed.

∎

F.3
Proof of Corollary
4.4

Proof of Corollary
4.4
.

We proceed similarly as in the proof of Corollary
F.4
in Section
F.2.2
:

Let
\varepsilon\in\mathbb{R}_{+}^{*}
.
Let us find
T
to ensure that
\mathbb{E}\min_{t\in\{1,..,T\}}(1-\rho)R({\bm{w}}_{t})+\rho R({\bm{v}}_{t})-R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\leq\varepsilon

This will be enforced if:

\displaystyle 2\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{\rho}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\omega^{T}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)\leq\varepsilon

\displaystyle\iff T\log(\omega)\leq\log\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{2\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{\rho}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle\iff T\geq\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{2\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{\rho}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right).

Therefore, let us take:

T:=\left\lceil\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{2\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{\rho}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right)\right\rceil.

(33)

We can now derive the #IFO and #HT. First, we have one hard-thresholding operation at each iteration, therefore #HT
=T
. Using the fact that
\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}=\frac{1}{-\log(\omega)}=\frac{1}{-\log(1-\frac{1}{4\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}})}\leq\frac{1}{\frac{1}{4\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}}}=4\alpha\frac{1}{\rho}\kappa_{s}
(since by property of the logarithm, for all
x\in(-\infty,-1):\log(1-x)\leq-x
), we obtain that
\text{\#HT}=\mathcal{O}(\kappa_{s}\log\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right))
.

We now turn to computing the #IFO. At each iteration
t
we have
s_{t}
gradient evaluations, therefore:

#IFO

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}s_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left(\frac{\tau}{\omega^{t}}+1\right)

\displaystyle=T+\tau\frac{\left(\frac{1}{\omega}\right)^{T}-1}{\frac{1}{\omega}-1}

\displaystyle\leq T+\frac{\tau}{{\frac{1}{\omega}-1}}\left(\frac{1}{\omega}\right)^{T}

\displaystyle=T+\frac{\tau}{{\frac{1}{\omega}-1}}\exp\left(T\log\left(\frac{1}{\omega}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}1+\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{2\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{\rho}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle~~~~~+\frac{\tau}{{\frac{1}{\omega}-1}}\exp\left(\log\left(\frac{1}{\omega}\right)\left[\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{2\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{\rho}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right)+1\right]\right)

\displaystyle=1+\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{2\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{\rho}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right)+\frac{\frac{\tau}{\omega}}{{\frac{1}{\omega}-1}}\frac{2\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{\rho}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=1+\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{2\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{\rho}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right)+\frac{\tau}{{1-\omega}}\frac{2\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{\rho}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=1+\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{2\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{\rho}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right)+\tau\frac{8\alpha^{3}\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}L_{s}\kappa_{s}^{2}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}1+\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{2\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{\rho}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle~~~~~+\frac{B}{\alpha L_{s}}\frac{1}{L_{s}(\alpha-1)}\frac{8\alpha^{3}\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}L_{s}}{\varepsilon}\frac{L_{s}}{\nu_{s}}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)

\displaystyle=1+\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{2\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{\rho}L_{s}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right)+\frac{8B\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\right)}{(\alpha-1)\nu_{s}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon},

where (a) follows from equation
33
, and for (b) we recall that
\tau=\frac{\eta B}{C}
,
\eta=\frac{1}{\alpha L_{s}}
and
C=L_{s}(\alpha-1)
.
Therefore, overall, the IFO complexity is in
\mathcal{O}(\frac{\kappa_{s}}{\nu_{s}\varepsilon})
.

∎

F.4
Proof of Theorems
4.7
and
4.8

Our proof for Theorem
4.8
is similar to the one for Theorem
4.3
, though we needed to refine some results from
(
de Vazelhes et al. 2022
)
to properly express the variance of the ZO gradient estimator and incorporate it into the telescopic sum.
Before proving the main Theorem
4.8
, below we provide several intermediary results needed for the proof of Theorem
4.8
. Then, the proof of Theorem
4.3
will be provided in Section
F.4.3
.

F.4.1
Useful Lemmas

We first recall the following results from
(
de Vazelhes et al. 2022
)
:

Proposition F.5

(Proposition 1 (i)
(
de Vazelhes et al. 2022
)
)
.

Let us consider any support
F\subseteq[d]
of size
s
(
|F|=s
). For the Z0 gradient estimator
{\bm{g}}_{t}
in Algorithm
3
at
{\bm{w}}_{t}
, with
q_{t}
random directions, and random supports of size
s_{2}
, and assuming that
R
is
(L_{s_{2}},s_{2})
-RSS’ , we have, with
[{\bm{u}}]_{F}
denoting the hard thresholding of a vector
{\bm{u}}
on
F
(that is, we set all coordinates not in
F
to
0
):

\|[\mathbb{E}{{\bm{g}}_{t}}]_{F}-[\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t})]_{F}\|^{2}\leq\varepsilon_{\mu}\mu^{2}

(34)

with
\varepsilon_{\mu}:=L_{s_{2}}^{2}sd

Proof of Proposition
F.5
.

Proof in
(
de Vazelhes et al. 2022
)
.
∎

Lemma F.6

(Lemma C.2
(
de Vazelhes et al. 2022
)
)
.

For any
(L_{s_{2}},s_{2})
-RSS’ function R, using the gradient estimator
{\bm{g}}_{t}
defined in Algorithm
3
with
q_{t}=1
, we have, for any support
F\subseteq[d]
, with
|F|=s
, and
F^{c}:=[d]\setminus F
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\|[{\bm{g}}_{t}]_{F}\|^{2}=\varepsilon_{F}\left\|[\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t})]_{F}\right\|^{2}+\varepsilon_{F^{c}}\left\|[\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t})]_{F^{c}}\right\|^{2}+\varepsilon_{\text{abs}}\mu^{2}

(35)

with:

(i)
\varepsilon_{F}:=\frac{2d}{(s_{2}+2)}\left(\frac{(s-1)(s_{2}-1)}{d-1}+3\right)

(ii)
\varepsilon_{F^{c}}:=\frac{2d}{(s_{2}+2)}\left(\frac{s(s_{2}-1)}{d-1}\right)

(iii)
\varepsilon_{\text{abs}}:=2dL_{s}^{2}ss_{2}\left(\frac{(s-1)(s_{2}-1)}{d-1}+1\right)
.

Proof of Lemma
F.6
.

Proof in
(
de Vazelhes et al. 2022
)
.
∎

We now use the above lemma to bound the variance of the zeroth-order gradient estimator
{\bm{g}}_{t}
.

Lemma F.7
.

The gradient estimator
{\bm{g}}_{t}
defined in Algorithm
3
verifies the following properties for any
q_{t}\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
:

\mathbb{E}\|[{\bm{g}}_{t}]_{F}-\mathbb{E}[{\bm{g}}_{t}]_{F}\|^{2}\leq\frac{\varepsilon_{F}}{q_{t}}\left\|\nabla R({\bm{w}})\right\|^{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{abs}}{q_{t}}\mu^{2}

(36)

with
\varepsilon_{F}
and
\varepsilon_{abs}
defined above in Lemma
F.6

Proof of Lemma
F.7
.

If
q_{t}=1
, we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\|[{\bm{g}}_{t}]_{F}-\mathbb{E}[{\bm{g}}_{t}]_{F}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}\mathbb{E}\|[{\bm{g}}_{t}]_{F}\|^{2}-\|[\mathbb{E}{\bm{g}}]_{F}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\|[{\bm{g}}_{t}]_{F}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(\ref{eq:gradbound})}{\leq}\varepsilon_{F}\|[\nabla R({\bm{w}})]_{F}\|^{2}+\varepsilon_{F^{c}}\|[\nabla R({\bm{w}})]_{F^{c}}\|^{2}+\varepsilon_{\text{abs}}\mu^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\varepsilon_{F}\|\nabla R({\bm{w}})\|^{2}+\varepsilon_{abs}\mu^{2},

where (a) follows from the bias-variance formula
\mathbb{E}\|X-E[X]\|_{2}^{2}=\mathbb{E}\|X\|_{2}^{2}-\|\mathbb{E}X\|_{2}^{2}
for a multidimensional random variable
X
, and (b) follows from the fact that

\varepsilon_{F}=\frac{2d}{s_{2}+2}\left(\frac{s(s_{2}-1)}{d-1}+3-\frac{s_{2}-1}{d}\right)>\frac{2d}{s_{2}+2}\left(\frac{s(s_{2}-1)}{d-1}\right)=\varepsilon_{F^{c}}

(since
s_{2}\leq d
), and since
\|[\nabla R({\bm{w}})]_{F}\|^{2}+\|[\nabla R({\bm{w}})]_{F^{c}}\|^{2}=\|\nabla R({\bm{w}})\|^{2}
(by definition of the Euclidean norm).

Now, if
q_{t}\geq 1
, we know that the variance of an average of
q_{t}
i.i.d. realizations of a random variable of total variance
\sigma^{2}
is
\frac{\sigma^{2}}{q_{t}}
(and its expected value remains the same by linearity of expectation): indeed, for any random multidimensional random variable
X
, for which we consider the
q
i.i.d. random variables
X_{i}
of same distribution, we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\frac{1}{q_{t}}\sum_{i=1}^{q_{t}}X_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{q_{t}}\sum_{i=1}^{q_{t}}X_{i}\right]\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\|\frac{1}{q_{t}}\sum_{i=1}^{q_{t}}\left(X_{i}-\mathbb{E}X_{i}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{q_{t}^{2}}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{q_{t}}\left(X_{i}-\mathbb{E}X_{i}\right)\right)^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{q_{t}}\left(X_{i}-\mathbb{E}X_{i}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}\frac{1}{q_{t}^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{q_{t}}\|X_{i}-\mathbb{E}X_{i}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{q_{t}^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{q_{t}}\|X-\mathbb{E}X\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{q_{t}^{2}}q_{t}\|X-\mathbb{E}X\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{q_{t}}\|X-\mathbb{E}X\|_{2}^{2},

where (a) follows from the fact that
X_{i}
are i.i.d hence for
i\neq j
:
\text{Cov}(X_{i},X_{j})=\mathbb{E}(X_{i}-\mathbb{E}X_{i})^{\top}(X_{j}-\mathbb{E}X_{j})=0
.
Applying this to the random variable which realizations are
[{\bm{g}}_{t}]_{F}
, this concludes the proof.
∎

F.4.2
Proof of Theorem
4.7

Below we now first present some results (and their proofs) for the convergence of Algorithm
3
without the additional constraint, which is needed for the proof of Theorem
4.8
, and also, as a byproduct, provides, up to our knowledge, the first convergence guarantee in objective value without system error for a zeroth-order hard-thresholding algorithm.

Proof of Theorem
4.7
.

Let us denote for simplicity:

C_{1}:=\frac{\varepsilon_{F}}{q_{t}}
,
C_{2}:=\frac{\varepsilon_{abs}}{q_{t}}
, and
C_{3}:=\varepsilon_{\mu}\mu^{2}
. Moreover, let us denote
F:=\text{\text{supp}}({\bm{w}}_{t})\cup\text{supp}({\bm{w}}_{t-1})\cup\text{supp}(\bar{{\bm{w}}})
, where
supp
denotes the support of a vector, i.e. the set of coordinates of its non-zero components. Note that therefore we have
|F|\leq 2k+\bar{k}\leq 3k
. In addition
[{\bm{u}}]_{F}
denotes the thresholding of
{\bm{u}}
to the support
F
, that is, the vector
{\bm{u}}
with its components that are not in
F
set to 0.

The fact that
R
is
(L_{s^{\prime}},s^{\prime})
-RSS’, therefore also
(L_{s^{\prime}},s)
-RSS’, implies from the remark in
4.5
that it is also
(L_{s^{\prime}},s)
-RSS, therefore:

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1}),{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle+\frac{L_{s^{\prime}}}{2}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\langle{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle+\frac{L_{s^{\prime}}}{2}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-({\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\eta{\bm{g}}_{t-1})\|^{2}-\eta^{2}\|{\bm{g}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}\right]+\frac{L_{s^{\prime}}}{2}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-({\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\eta{\bm{g}}_{t-1})\|^{2}-\frac{\eta}{2}\|{\bm{g}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\left[\frac{L_{s^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{\eta}}{2}\right]\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\langle[\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1}]_{F},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-({\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\eta{\bm{g}}_{t-1})\|^{2}-(1-\sqrt{\beta})\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}\right]-\frac{\eta}{2}\|{\bm{g}}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left[\frac{L_{s^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{\eta}}{2}\right]\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\langle[\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1}]_{F},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\eta^{2}\|{\bm{g}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-2\langle\eta{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\rangle\right]-\frac{1}{2\eta}(1-\sqrt{\beta})\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{\eta}{2}\|{\bm{g}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\left[\frac{L_{s^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{\eta}}{2}\right]\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\langle[\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1}]_{F},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-2\langle\eta{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\rangle\right]-\frac{1}{2\eta}(1-\sqrt{\beta})\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left[\frac{L_{s^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{\eta}}{2}\right]\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\langle[\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1}]_{F},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\langle{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\rangle-\frac{1}{2\eta}(1-\sqrt{\beta})\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left[\frac{L_{s^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{\eta}+C}{2}\right]\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2C}\|[\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1}]_{F}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1}),{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\rangle+\langle[\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1}]_{F},{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\eta}(1-\sqrt{\beta})\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}+\left[\frac{L_{s^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{\eta}+C}{2}\right]\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2C}\|[\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1}]_{F}\|^{2},

where (a) follows from Lemma
3.2
and (b) follows from the inequality
\langle a,b\rangle\leq\frac{C}{2}a^{2}+\frac{1}{2C}b^{2}
, for any
(a,b)\in(\mathbb{R}^{d})^{2}
with
C>0
an arbitrary strictly positive constant.

Let us now choose
\eta:=\frac{1}{L_{s^{\prime}}+C}
: therefore the term
\left[\frac{L_{s^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{\eta}+C}{2}\right]\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}
above is
0
. We now take the conditional expectation (conditioned on
{\textnormal{w}}_{t-1}
, which is the random variable which realizations are
{\bm{w}}_{t-1}
), on both sides, and from Lemma
F.3

we obtain the inequality below (we slightly abuse notations and denote
\mathbb{E}[\cdot|{\textnormal{w}}_{t-1}={\bm{w}}_{t-1}]
by
\mathbb{E}[\cdot|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}]
):

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[R({\bm{w}}_{t})|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1}),{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\eta}(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]+\langle[\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{g}}_{t-1}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}]\right]_{F},{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\rangle

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{2C}\|[\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1}]_{F}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1}),{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\eta}(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]+\frac{G}{2}\|[\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-\mathbb{E}[{\bm{g}}_{t-1}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}]]_{F}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2G}\|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2C}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\left[\frac{1}{2\eta}+\frac{1}{2G}\right]\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1}),{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\eta}(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]+\frac{G}{2}\|[\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-\mathbb{E}[{\bm{g}}_{t-1}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}]]_{F}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2C}\mathbb{E}\left[\|[\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1}]_{F}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\left[\frac{1}{2\eta}+\frac{1}{2G}\right]\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1}),{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\eta}(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]+\frac{G}{2}\|[\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-\mathbb{E}[{\bm{g}}_{t-1}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}]]_{F}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2C}\left(2\|[\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-\mathbb{E}[{\bm{g}}_{t-1}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}]]_{F}\|^{2}+2\|[{\bm{g}}_{t-1}-\mathbb{E}[{\bm{g}}_{t-1}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}]]_{F}\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(\ref{eq:bias})+(\ref{eq:variance})}{\leq}

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\left[\frac{1}{2\eta}+\frac{1}{2G}\right]\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1}),{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\eta}(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]+\frac{G}{2}C_{3}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2C}\left(2C_{3}+2C_{1}\|\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})\|^{2}+2C_{2}\mu^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\left[\frac{1}{2\eta}+\frac{1}{2G}\right]\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1}),{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\eta}(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]+\frac{G}{2}C_{3}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2C}\left(2C_{1}\left(2\|\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}+2\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}\right)+2C_{2}\mu^{2}+2C_{3}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\left[\frac{1}{2\eta}+\frac{1}{2G}\right]\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1}),{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\eta}(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]+\frac{G}{2}C_{3}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2C}\left(2C_{1}\left(2L_{s^{\prime}}^{2}\|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}+2\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}\right)+2C_{2}\mu^{2}+2C_{3}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\left[\frac{1}{2\eta}+\frac{1}{2G}+\frac{2C_{1}L_{s^{\prime}}^{2}}{C}\right]\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1}),{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\eta}(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]+\frac{G}{2}C_{3}+\frac{1}{C}\left(2C_{1}\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}+C_{2}\mu^{2}+C_{3}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(e)}{\leq}

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\left[\frac{1}{2\eta}+\frac{1}{2G}+\frac{2C_{1}L_{s^{\prime}}^{2}}{C}\right]\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\left[R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})-R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-\frac{\nu_{s}}{2}\|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\eta}(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]+\frac{G}{2}C_{3}+\frac{1}{C}\left(2C_{1}\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}+C_{2}\mu^{2}+C_{3}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})+\left[\frac{\frac{1}{\eta}-\nu_{s}}{2}+\frac{1}{2G}+\frac{2C_{1}L_{s^{\prime}}^{2}}{C}\right]\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\eta}(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]+\frac{G}{2}C_{3}+\frac{1}{C}\left(2C_{1}\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}+C_{2}\mu^{2}+C_{3}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(f)}{\leq}

\displaystyle R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})+\left[\frac{\frac{1}{\eta}-\nu_{s}}{2}+\frac{1}{2G}+\frac{2\varepsilon_{F}L_{s^{\prime}}^{2}}{\tau C}\right]\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\eta}(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]+\frac{G}{2}C_{3}+\frac{1}{C}\left(2C_{1}\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}+C_{2}\mu^{2}+C_{3}\right),

(37)

where (a) follows from the inequality
\langle a,b\rangle\leq\frac{G}{2}a^{2}+\frac{1}{2G}b^{2}
, for any
(a,b)\in(\mathbb{R}^{d})^{2}
with
G>0
an arbitrary strictly positive constant,
(b) and (c) follow from the inequality
\|a+b\|^{2}\leq 2\|a\|^{2}+2\|b\|^{2}
for any
(a,b)\in(\mathbb{R}^{d})^{2}
, (d) follows from the fact that
R
is
(L_{s^{\prime}},s^{\prime})
-RSS’ (Assumption
4.5
with sparsity level
s^{\prime}
), therefore it is also (
L_{s^{\prime}}
,
s_{2}
)-RSS’, (e) follows from the RSC condition, and for (f), we recall that
C_{1}=\frac{\varepsilon_{F}}{q_{t}}
, and we define
q_{t}=\left\lceil\frac{\tau}{\omega^{t}}\right\rceil
, for some
\omega>1
and
\tau>0
that will be chosen later in the proof.

Recall that we have chosen
\eta=\frac{1}{L_{s^{\prime}}+C}
.
Let us define
\alpha:=\frac{C}{L_{s^{\prime}}}+1
. Then
C=(\alpha-1)L_{s^{\prime}}
, and
\eta=\frac{1}{\alpha L_{s^{\prime}}}
. Also recall that
\kappa_{s}=\frac{L_{s^{\prime}}}{\nu_{s}}
.

We will now choose the constant
G
and
C
, in order to simplify the inequality above, such that it matches as much as possible the structure of the previous proofs:

We will seek to rewrite:

\left[\frac{\frac{1}{\eta}-\nu_{s}}{2}+\frac{1}{2G}+\frac{2\frac{\varepsilon_{F}}{\tau}L_{s^{\prime}}^{2}}{C}\right]\left(=\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[1+\frac{1}{G\alpha L_{s^{\prime}}}+\frac{4L_{s^{\prime}}^{2}\frac{\varepsilon_{F}}{\tau}}{(\alpha-1)\alpha L_{s^{\prime}}^{2}}-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}\right]\right)
, into :

\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}\right]
for some
\alpha^{\prime}>0
(we will seek
\alpha^{\prime}\propto\alpha
, with a dimensionless proportionality constant for simplicity).

Therefore, let us choose
G:=\frac{4}{\nu_{s}}
, which implies:

\frac{1}{G\alpha L_{s^{\prime}}}=\frac{1}{4\alpha\kappa_{s}}.

(38)

And let us choose
\tau:=\frac{16\kappa_{s}\varepsilon_{F}}{(\alpha-1)}
, which implies:

\frac{4L_{s^{\prime}}^{2}\frac{\varepsilon_{F}}{\tau}}{(\alpha-1)\alpha L_{s^{\prime}}^{2}}=\frac{1}{4\alpha\kappa_{s}}.

(39)

Therefore, using equations
38
and
39
, we obtain:

\displaystyle\left[\frac{\frac{1}{\eta}-\nu_{s}}{2}+\frac{1}{2G}+\frac{2\frac{\varepsilon_{F}}{\tau}L_{s^{\prime}}^{2}}{C}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[1+\frac{1}{G\alpha L_{s^{\prime}}}+\frac{4L_{s^{\prime}}^{2}\frac{\varepsilon_{F}}{\tau}}{(\alpha-1)\alpha L_{s^{\prime}}^{2}}-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[1+\frac{1}{4\alpha\kappa_{s}}+\frac{1}{4\alpha\kappa_{s}}-\frac{1}{\alpha\kappa_{s}}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[1-\frac{1}{2\alpha\kappa_{s}}\right]=\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}\right],

where for simplicity we have denoted
\alpha^{\prime}=2\alpha
.
We can therefore simplify (
37
) into:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[R({\bm{w}}_{t})|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}]-R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\left(1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}\right)\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\left.+2\eta\left(\frac{G}{2}C_{3}+\frac{1}{C}\left(2C_{1}\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}+C_{2}\mu^{2}+C_{3}\right)\right)\right].

We now take the expectation over
{\textnormal{w}}_{t-1}
of the above inequality (i.e. we take
\mathbb{E}_{{\textnormal{w}}_{t-1}}[\cdot]
): using the law of total expectation (
\mathbb{E}[\cdot]=\mathbb{E}_{{\textnormal{w}}_{t-1}}[\mathbb{E}[\cdot|{\bm{w}}_{t-1}]]
) we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}R({\bm{w}}_{t})-R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\left(1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}\right)\mathbb{E}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}\right.

(40)

\displaystyle\left.+2\eta\left(\frac{G}{2}C_{3}+\frac{1}{C}\left(2C_{1}\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}+C_{2}\mu^{2}+C_{3}\right)\right)\right]

(41)

Let us call
A:=2\eta\left(\frac{G}{2}C_{3}+\frac{1}{C}\left(2C_{1}\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}+C_{2}\mu^{2}+C_{3}\right)\right)
for simplicity.
Similarly as in
(
Liu & Foygel Barber 2020
)
, we now take a weighted sum over
t=1,...,T
, to obtain:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}2\eta\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\mathbb{E}[R({\bm{w}}_{t})-R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\left[\left(1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}\right)\mathbb{E}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}+A\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\left[\left(1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}\right)\mathbb{E}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-(1-\sqrt{\beta})\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}A

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\sqrt{\beta})\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t+1}\mathbb{E}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}A

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle(1-\sqrt{\beta})\left[\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}-\mathbb{E}\|{\bm{w}}_{T}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}A

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\sqrt{\beta})\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}A

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}A

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}2\eta\left(\frac{G}{2}C_{3}+\frac{1}{C}\left(2C_{1}\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}+C_{2}\mu^{2}+C_{3}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}2\eta\left(\frac{G}{2}C_{3}+\frac{1}{C}\left(2\frac{\varepsilon_{F}}{q_{t}}\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{abs}\mu^{2}}{q_{t}}+C_{3}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\frac{2\eta}{q_{t}}\left(\frac{2\varepsilon_{F}\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}+\varepsilon_{abs}\mu^{2}}{C}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}2\eta C_{3}\left(\frac{G}{2}+\frac{1}{C}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\frac{2\eta}{q_{t}}\left(\frac{2\varepsilon_{F}\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}}{C}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}2\eta\mu^{2}\left(\varepsilon_{\mu}\left(\frac{G}{2}+\frac{1}{C}\right)+\frac{\varepsilon_{abs}}{Cq_{t}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\frac{2\eta}{q_{t}}\left(\frac{2\varepsilon_{F}\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}}{C}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}2\eta\mu^{2}\left(\varepsilon_{\mu}\left(\frac{G}{2}+\frac{1}{C}\right)+\frac{\varepsilon_{abs}}{C}\right),

(42)

where (a) follows from simplifying the telescopic sum.
Let us denote for simplicity
\zeta:=\frac{2\eta(2\varepsilon_{F}\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2})}{C}=\frac{4\eta\varepsilon_{F}\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}}{C}
and
Z:=\varepsilon_{\mu}\left(\frac{G}{2}+\frac{1}{C}\right)+\frac{\varepsilon_{abs}}{C}
.

We now choose
k
and
q_{t}
as follows: we choose
k\geq 4\alpha^{\prime 2}\kappa_{s}^{2}\bar{k}
, which implies that:

\displaystyle\sqrt{\beta}\leq\frac{1}{2\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}

\displaystyle\implies\sqrt{\beta}\leq\frac{1}{2\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}-1}

\displaystyle\implies 1-\sqrt{\beta}\geq 1-\frac{1}{2\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}-1}=\frac{2\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}-2}{2\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}-1}=\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\frac{1}{2\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}

\displaystyle\implies\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)\leq 1-\frac{1}{2\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}.

(43)

We recall that we previously defined
q_{t}=\left\lceil\frac{\tau}{\omega^{t}}\right\rceil
, with
\tau:=\frac{16\kappa_{s}\varepsilon_{F}}{(\alpha-1)}
. We now set the value of
\omega
, to
\omega:=1-\frac{1}{4\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}
.

Let us call
\nu:=1-\frac{1}{2\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}
. Note that we have:

\nu\leq\omega.

(44)

And that we have the inequality below:

\frac{\nu}{\omega}=\frac{1-\frac{1}{2\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\frac{1}{4\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}=\frac{4\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}-2}{4\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}-1}=1-\frac{1}{4\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}-1}\leq 1-\frac{1}{4\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}=\omega.

(45)

This allows us to simplify equation
42
into:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}2\eta\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}[R({\bm{w}}_{t})-R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})]\right]

\displaystyle\leq\nu^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{\zeta}{\tau}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\nu^{T-t}\omega^{t-1}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}2\eta Z\mu^{2}

\displaystyle=\nu^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{\zeta}{\tau}\frac{\omega^{T}}{\omega}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{\nu}{\omega}\right)^{T-t}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}2\eta Z\mu^{2}

\displaystyle=\nu^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{\zeta}{\tau}\frac{\omega^{T}}{\omega}\frac{1-\left(\frac{\nu}{\omega}\right)^{T}}{1-\left(\frac{\nu}{\omega}\right)}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}2\eta Z\mu^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\nu^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{\zeta}{\tau}\frac{\omega^{T}}{\omega}\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{\nu}{\omega}\right)}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}2\eta Z\mu^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\nu^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{\zeta}{\tau}\frac{\omega^{T}}{\omega}\frac{1}{1-\omega}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}2\eta Z\mu^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\nu^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{\zeta}{\tau}\frac{4}{3}\omega^{T}\frac{1}{1-\omega}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}2\eta Z\mu^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}\omega^{T}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{\zeta}{\tau}\frac{4}{3}\omega^{T}\frac{1}{1-\omega}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}2\eta Z\mu^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}\frac{\omega^{T}}{1-\omega}\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{\zeta}{\tau}\frac{4}{3}\omega^{T}\frac{1}{1-\omega}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}2\eta Z\mu^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\omega^{T}}{1-\omega}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{\zeta}{\tau}\frac{4}{3}\right)+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}2\eta Z\mu^{2}

\displaystyle=4\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}\omega^{T}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{\zeta}{\tau}\frac{4}{3}\right)+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}2\eta Z\mu^{2},

where in the left hand side we have used the linearity of expectation, and where (a) uses equation
45
,
(b) uses the fact that
\frac{1}{\omega}=\frac{1}{1-\frac{1}{4\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}\leq\frac{1}{1-\frac{1}{4}}=\frac{4}{3}
(since
\kappa_{s}\geq 1
and
\alpha^{\prime}\geq 1
(indeed, we have
\alpha^{\prime}=2\alpha=2(\frac{C}{L_{s^{\prime}}}+1)
with
C>0
)), (c) uses equation
44
, and (d) uses the fact that
\omega<1
so
1<\frac{1}{1-\omega}
.

Let us now normalize the above inequality:

\mathbb{E}\frac{\sum_{t=1}^{T}2\eta\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}R({\bm{w}}_{t})}{\sum_{t=1}^{T}2\eta\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}}\leq R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})+\frac{4\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}\omega^{T}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\frac{\zeta}{\tau}\right)}{\sum_{t=1}^{T}2\eta\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}}+Z\mu^{2}.

The left hand side above is a weighted sum, which is an upper bound on the smallest term of the sum.

Regarding the right hand side, we can simplify it using the fact that
0<\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)
, and therefore:

\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1-\frac{1}{\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}}{1-\sqrt{\beta}}\right)^{T-t}\geq 1.

Therefore, we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\min_{t\in\{1,..,T\}}R({\bm{w}}_{t})-R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}\omega^{T}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\frac{\zeta}{\tau}\right)}{2\eta}+Z\mu^{2}

\displaystyle=4\alpha^{2}L_{s^{\prime}}\kappa_{s}\omega^{T}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\frac{\zeta}{\tau}\right)+Z\mu^{2}.

Which can be simplified into the expression below, using the definition of
\hat{{\bm{w}}}_{T}
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}R(\hat{{\bm{w}}}_{T})-R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})

\displaystyle\leq 4\alpha^{2}L_{s^{\prime}}\kappa_{s}\omega^{T}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\frac{\zeta}{\tau}\right)+Z\mu^{2}.

(46)

To simplify the above result, we recall the assumptions made earlier on:
we have chosen

\tau=\frac{16\kappa_{s}\varepsilon_{F}}{(\alpha-1)}
, and
G=\frac{4}{\nu_{s}}
.

Therefore, to sum up, we have:

Z=\varepsilon_{\mu}\left(\frac{G}{2}+\frac{1}{C}\right)+\frac{\varepsilon_{abs}}{C}=\varepsilon_{\mu}\left(\frac{2}{\nu_{s}}+\frac{1}{C}\right)+\frac{\varepsilon_{abs}}{C}.

\omega=1-\frac{1}{4\alpha^{\prime}\kappa_{s}}=1-\frac{1}{8\alpha\kappa_{s}}

\zeta=\frac{4\eta\varepsilon_{F}\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}}{C}

The last inequality implies:
\frac{\zeta}{\tau}=\frac{\frac{4\eta\varepsilon_{F}\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}}{C}}{16\kappa_{s}L_{s^{\prime}}\frac{\varepsilon_{F}}{C}}=\frac{\eta\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}}{4\kappa_{s}L_{s^{\prime}}}
.

∎

Corollary F.8
.

Additionally, the number of calls to the function
R
(#IZO), and the number of hard thresholding operations (#HT) such that the upper bound in Theorem
4.3
above is smaller than
\varepsilon+Z\mu
, with
\varepsilon>0
are respectively:

\text{\#HT}=\mathcal{O}(\kappa_{s}\log(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}))~~~\text{and}~~~\text{\#IZO}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\varepsilon_{F}\kappa_{s}^{3}L_{s}}{\varepsilon}\right)
. Note that if
s_{2}=d
, we have
\varepsilon_{F}=\mathcal{O}(s)=\mathcal{O}(k)
, and therefore we obtain a query complexity that is dimension independent.

Proof of Corollary
F.8
.

Let
\varepsilon\in\mathbb{R}_{+}^{*}
.
Let us find
T
to ensure that
\mathbb{E}R(\hat{{\bm{w}}}_{T})-R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\leq\varepsilon+Z\mu^{2}

This will be enforced if:

\displaystyle 4\alpha^{2}L_{s^{\prime}}\kappa_{s}\omega^{T}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\frac{\eta\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}}{4\kappa_{s}L_{s^{\prime}}}\right)\leq\varepsilon

\displaystyle\iff T\log(\omega)\leq\log\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{4\alpha^{2}L_{s^{\prime}}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\frac{\eta\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}}{4\kappa_{s}L_{s^{\prime}}}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle\iff T\geq\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{4\alpha^{2}L_{s^{\prime}}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\frac{\eta\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}}{4\kappa_{s}L_{s^{\prime}}}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right).

Therefore, let us take:

T:=\left\lceil\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{4\alpha^{2}L_{s^{\prime}}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\frac{\eta\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}}{4\kappa_{s}L_{s^{\prime}}}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right)\right\rceil.

(47)

We can now derive the #IZO and #HT. First, we have one hard-thresholding operation at each iteration, therefore #HT
=T
. Using the fact that
\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}=\frac{1}{-\log(\omega)}=\frac{1}{-\log(1-\frac{1}{8\alpha\kappa_{s}})}\leq\frac{1}{\frac{1}{8\alpha\kappa_{s}}}=8\alpha\kappa_{s}
(since by property of the logarithm, for all
x\in(-\infty,-1):\log(1-x)\leq-x
), and the fact that
\alpha=\frac{C}{L_{s^{\prime}}}
is independent of
\kappa_{s}
, we obtain that
\text{\#HT}=\mathcal{O}(\kappa_{s}\log\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right))
.

We now turn to computing the #IZO. At each iteration
t
we have
q_{t}
function evaluations, therefore:

\displaystyle\text{\#IFO}=\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}q_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left(\frac{\tau}{\omega^{t}}+1\right)

\displaystyle=T+\tau\frac{\left(\frac{1}{\omega}\right)^{T}-1}{\frac{1}{\omega}-1}

\displaystyle\leq T+\frac{\tau}{{\frac{1}{\omega}-1}}\left(\frac{1}{\omega}\right)^{T}

\displaystyle=T+\frac{\tau}{{\frac{1}{\omega}-1}}\exp\left(T\log\left(\frac{1}{\omega}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}1+\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{4\alpha^{2}L_{s^{\prime}}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\frac{\eta\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}}{4\kappa_{s}L_{s^{\prime}}}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle~~~~~+\frac{\tau}{{\frac{1}{\omega}-1}}\exp\left(\log\left(\frac{1}{\omega}\right)\left[\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{4\alpha^{2}L_{s^{\prime}}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\frac{\eta\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}}{4\kappa_{s}L_{s^{\prime}}}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right)+1\right]\right)

\displaystyle=1+\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{4\alpha^{2}L_{s^{\prime}}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\frac{\eta\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}}{4\kappa_{s}L_{s^{\prime}}}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right)+\frac{\frac{\tau}{\omega}}{{\frac{1}{\omega}-1}}\frac{4\alpha^{2}L_{s^{\prime}}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\frac{\eta\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}}{4\kappa_{s}L_{s^{\prime}}}\right)}{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=1+\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{4\alpha^{2}L_{s^{\prime}}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\frac{\eta\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}}{4\kappa_{s}L_{s^{\prime}}}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right)+\frac{\tau}{{1-\omega}}\frac{4\alpha^{2}L_{s^{\prime}}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\frac{\eta\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}}{4\kappa_{s}L_{s^{\prime}}}\right)}{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=1+\frac{1}{\log(\frac{1}{\omega})}\log\left(\frac{4\alpha^{2}L_{s^{\prime}}\kappa_{s}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\frac{\eta\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}}{4\kappa_{s}L_{s^{\prime}}}\right)}{\varepsilon}\right)+\tau\frac{32\alpha^{3}L_{s^{\prime}}\kappa_{s}^{2}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\frac{\eta\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}}{4\kappa_{s}L_{s^{\prime}}}\right)}{\varepsilon},

where (a) follows from equation
47
.

And we recall that
\tau:=\frac{16\kappa_{s}\varepsilon_{F}}{(\alpha-1)}
, which implies that:

\tau\frac{32\alpha^{3}L_{s^{\prime}}\kappa_{s}^{2}\left(\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\frac{\eta\|\nabla R(\bar{{\bm{w}}})\|^{2}}{2\gamma\kappa_{s}L_{s^{\prime}}}\right)}{\varepsilon}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\varepsilon_{F}}{\varepsilon}\left(\kappa_{s}^{3}L_{s^{\prime}}+\frac{\kappa_{s}}{\nu_{s}}\right)\right).

Therefore, overall, the # IZO complexity is in
\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\varepsilon_{F}}{\varepsilon}\kappa_{s}^{3}L_{s^{\prime}}\right)
.

∎

F.4.3
Proof of Theorem
4.8

Using the results above, we can now proceed to the proof of Theorem
4.8
.

Proof of Theorem
4.8
.

Let us denote for simplicity:

C_{1}:=\frac{\varepsilon_{F}}{q_{t}}
,
C_{2}:=\frac{\varepsilon_{abs}}{q_{t}}
, and
C_{3}:=\varepsilon_{\mu}\mu^{2}
. Moreover, let us denote
F:=\text{supp}({\bm{w}}_{t})\cup\text{supp}({\bm{w}}_{t-1})\cup\text{supp}(\bar{{\bm{w}}})
, where
supp
denotes the support of a vector, i.e. the set of coordinates of its non-zero components. Note that therefore we have
|F|\leq 2k+\bar{k}\leq 3k
. In addition
[{\bm{u}}]_{F}
denotes the thresholding of
{\bm{u}}
to the support
F
, that is, the vector
{\bm{u}}
with its components that are not in
F
set to 0.
Since
R
is
L_{s^{\prime}}
-RSS’, with
s^{\prime}=\max(s_{2},s)
,
R
is also
s
-RSS’ and
s_{2}
-RSS’, with Lipschitz constant
L_{s^{\prime}}
.

Denote
{\bm{v}}_{t}=\mathcal{H}_{k}({\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\eta\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1}))
for any
t\in\mathbb{N}
. The fact that
R
is
(L_{s^{\prime}},s^{\prime})
-RSS’, therefore also
(L_{s^{\prime}},s)
-RSS’, implies from the remark in Assumption
4.5
that it is also
(L_{s^{\prime}},s)
-RSS, therefore:

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1}),{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle+\frac{L_{s}}{2}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\langle{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle+\frac{L_{s}}{2}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-({\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\eta{\bm{g}}_{t-1})\|^{2}-\eta^{2}\|{\bm{g}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}\right]+\frac{L_{s}}{2}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\langle\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-({\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\eta{\bm{g}}_{t-1})\|^{2}-\frac{\eta}{2}\|{\bm{g}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\left[\frac{L_{s}-\frac{1}{\eta}}{2}\right]\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\langle[\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1}]_{F},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-({\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\eta{\bm{g}}_{t-1})\|^{2}-\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}+\sqrt{\beta}\|{\bm{v}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}\right]-\frac{\eta}{2}\|{\bm{g}}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left[\frac{L_{s}-\frac{1}{\eta}}{2}\right]\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\langle[\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1}]_{F},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})+\frac{1}{2\eta}\left[\|\bar{{\bm{w}}}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\eta^{2}\|{\bm{g}}_{t-1}\|^{2}-2\langle\eta{\bm{g}}_{t-1},{\bm{w}}_{t-1}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\rangle\right]-\frac{1}{2\eta}\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}+\frac{\sqrt{\beta}}{2\eta}\|{\bm{v}}_{t}-\bar{{\bm{w}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{\eta}{2}\|{\bm{g}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\left[\frac{L_{s}-\frac{1}{\eta}}{2}\right]\|{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\langle[\nabla R({\bm{w}}_{t-1})-{\bm{g}}_{t-1}]_{F},{\bm{w}}_{t}-{\bm{w}}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle=
